def _grade_rows(did, subject):
    grades=sorted(g for g,v in FORMULA_LIBRARY.items() if subject in v)
    rows=[]
    for i in range(0,len(grades),3):
        rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton(f'{g} класс',callback_data=f'hw2_grade|{did}|{subject}|{g}') for g in grades[i:i+3]])
    rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton('⬅️ К заданию',callback_data=f'hw2_step1|{did}')])
    return InlineKeyboardMarkup(rows)


async def _show_step2(q,did):
    d=_draft(did)
    if not ((d['description'] or '').strip() or _attachments(did=did)):
        await q.answer('Сначала добавьте текст, фото или файл.',show_alert=True); return await _show_step1(q,did)
    sg=d['student_grade'] or '—'; subj=d['subject'] or 'PHYSICS'
    text=_draft_text(d,2)+f'\n\nУченик: {sg} класс\nВыберите класс материала. Текущий класс — только рекомендация; можно выбрать прошлые годы.'
    rows=[]
    if d['student_grade'] and int(d['student_grade']) in FORMULA_LIBRARY and subj in FORMULA_LIBRARY[int(d['student_grade'])]:
        rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton(f'🎓 {d["student_grade"]} класс — текущий',callback_data=f'hw2_grade|{did}|{subj}|{d["student_grade"]}')])
    rows += _grade_rows(did,subj).inline_keyboard[:-1]
    rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton('🔎 Найти тему',callback_data=f'hw2_topic_search|{did}')])
    rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton('💜 Определить тему с Аделией',callback_data=f'hw2_ai_topic|{did}')])
    rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton('⬅️ Назад',callback_data=f'hw2_step1|{did}')])
    await q.edit_message_text(text,reply_markup=InlineKeyboardMarkup(rows))


async def _show_topics(q,did,subject,grade):
    topics=FORMULA_LIBRARY.get(int(grade),{}).get(subject,{})
    rows=[]
    for key,item in topics.items():
        rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton((item.get('title') or key)[:55],callback_data=f'hw2_topic|{did}|{subject}|{grade}|{key}')])
    rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton('⬅️ К классам',callback_data=f'hw2_step2|{did}')])
    await q.edit_message_text(_draft_text(_draft(did),2)+f'\n\n🎓 {grade} класс\nВыберите тему:',reply_markup=InlineKeyboardMarkup(rows))


async def _show_step3(q,did):
    d=_draft(did)
    if not d['topic_key']: return await _show_step2(q,did)
    title=_topic_title(d['topic_grade'],d['topic_subject'],d['topic_key'])
    selected=json.loads(d['formula_topics_json'] or '[]')
    mark='✅' if d['formula_decision']==1 else '⬜'
    text=_draft_text(d,3)+f'\n\n📚 {d["topic_grade"]} класс • {title}\n\nДобавить формулы к этому ДЗ?\nСейчас: {mark} ' + ('добавляем' if d['formula_decision']==1 else 'не выбрано' if d['formula_decision'] is None else 'без формул')
    rows=[
      [InlineKeyboardButton('🔁 Добавить тему на повторение',callback_data=f'hw2_repeat_grades|{did}')],
      [InlineKeyboardButton('✅ Добавить формулы',callback_data=f'hw2_formula_yes|{did}'),InlineKeyboardButton('⬜ Без формул',callback_data=f'hw2_formula_no|{did}')],
    ]
    if d['formula_decision']==1:
        rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton(('☑ ' if selected else '☐ ')+title[:40],callback_data=f'hw2_formula_toggle|{did}|{d["topic_grade"]}|{d["topic_subject"]}|{d["topic_key"]}')])
        rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton('🔎 Найти формулу',callback_data=f'hw2_formula_search|{did}')])
        rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton('🎓 Добавить из другого класса',callback_data=f'hw2_formula_grades|{did}')])
    rows += [[InlineKeyboardButton('⬅️ Назад',callback_data=f'hw2_step2|{did}'),InlineKeyboardButton('➡️ Далее',callback_data=f'hw2_step4|{did}')]]
    await q.edit_message_text(text,reply_markup=InlineKeyboardMarkup(rows))


async def _show_step4(q,did):
    d=_draft(did)
    if d['formula_decision'] is None:
        await q.answer('Выберите: добавить формулы или без формул.',show_alert=True); return await _show_step3(q,did)

      [InlineKeyboardButton('⬅️ Назад',callback_data=f'hw2_step3|{did}')],
    ]
    current=d['deadline'] or 'не выбран'
    await q.edit_message_text(_draft_text(d,4)+f'\n\nТекущий срок: {current}\n\nВыберите срок:',reply_markup=InlineKeyboardMarkup(rows))


def _next_lesson_deadline(user_id, lesson_id=None):
    c=DB.connect()
    try:
        params=[int(user_id),datetime.now().isoformat()]
        sql="SELECT start_time FROM lessons WHERE user_id=? AND start_time>? AND lesson_status NOT IN ('CANCELLED','COMPLETED')"
        if lesson_id:
            sql+=' AND id<>?';params.append(int(lesson_id))
        row=c.execute(sql+' ORDER BY start_time LIMIT 1',params).fetchone()
        return row['start_time'] if row else None
    finally:c.close()

    d=_draft(did); _touch(did,review_ready=1); d=_draft(did)

    title=_topic_title(d['topic_grade'],d['topic_subject'],d['topic_key']) if d['topic_key'] else '—'; repeats=json.loads(d['repeat_topics_json'] or '[]')
    lines=[_draft_text(d,5),'','━━━━━━━━━━━━',f'👤 {u["name"] if u else "Ученик"}',f'📚 Тема: {d["topic_grade"]} класс • {title}',f'🔁 Тем на повторение: {len(repeats)}',f'📝 Задание: {(d["description"] or "только вложение")[:500]}',f'📎 Вложений: {len(at)}',f'🧮 Формулы: {len(formulas) if d["formula_decision"] else 0}',f'📅 Срок: {"без срока" if d["deadline"]=="NO_DEADLINE" else (d["deadline"] or "не выбран")}','━━━━━━━━━━━━']
    rows=[[InlineKeyboardButton('✅ Отправить ученику',callback_data=f'hw2_send|{did}')],[InlineKeyboardButton('✏️ Задание',callback_data=f'hw2_step1|{did}'),InlineKeyboardButton('📚 Тема',callback_data=f'hw2_step2|{did}')],[InlineKeyboardButton('🧮 Формулы',callback_data=f'hw2_step3|{did}'),InlineKeyboardButton('📅 Срок',callback_data=f'hw2_step4|{did}')],[InlineKeyboardButton('❌ Отмена',callback_data=f'hw2_cancel|{did}')]]
    await q.edit_message_text('\n'.join(lines),reply_markup=InlineKeyboardMarkup(rows))


async def callback(update:Update, context:ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    q=update.callback_query; data=q.data or ''
    await q.answer()
    if data.startswith('homework_view|'):
        hid=int(data.split('|')[1]); return await _student_homework_panel(q,hid)
    if data.startswith('admin_homework|') or data.startswith('admin_homework_create|'):
        if not _is_admin(update): return
        lesson_id=int(data.split('|')[1]); lesson=DB.get_lesson(lesson_id)
        if not lesson: return await q.answer('Занятие не найдено',show_alert=True)
        did=_new_draft(update.effective_user.id,lesson['user_id'],lesson_id)
        return await _show_step1(q,did)
    if not data.startswith('hw2_'): return
    if data.startswith('hw2_do|'):
        hid=int(data.split('|')[1]); hw=DB.get_homework(hid); user=DB.get_user_by_telegram_id(update.effective_user.id) if update.effective_user else None
        if not hw or not user or int(hw['user_id'])!=int(user['id']): return
        at=_attachments(hid=hid)
        if at:
            a=at[0]
            if a['file_type']=='photo': await context.bot.send_photo(update.effective_chat.id,a['file_id'],caption=a['file_name'] or '📎 Задание')
            else: await context.bot.send_document(update.effective_chat.id,a['file_id'],caption=a['file_name'] or '📎 Задание')
            return await _student_controls(context,update.effective_chat.id,hid)
        return await _student_formula_panel(q,hid)
    if data.startswith('hw2_openfile|'):
        parts=data.split('|'); hid=int(parts[1]); aid=int(parts[2]); hw=DB.get_homework(hid)
        user=DB.get_user_by_telegram_id(update.effective_user.id) if update.effective_user else None
        if not hw or not user or int(hw['user_id'])!=int(user['id']): return
        c=DB.connect(); a=c.execute('SELECT * FROM homework_attachments WHERE id=? AND homework_id=?',(aid,hid)).fetchone();c.close()
        if not a:return
        if a['file_type']=='photo': await context.bot.send_photo(update.effective_chat.id,a['file_id'],caption=a['file_name'] or '📎 Задание')
        else: await context.bot.send_document(update.effective_chat.id,a['file_id'],caption=a['file_name'] or '📎 Задание')
        return await _student_controls(context,update.effective_chat.id,hid)
    if data.startswith('hw2_student_formula|'):
        hid=int(data.split('|')[1]); return await _student_formula_panel(q,hid)
    if data.startswith('hw2_student_formula_card|'):
        _,hid,g,s,t=data.split('|',4); return await _student_formula_card(q,int(hid),int(g),s,t)
    if data.startswith('hw2_student_formula_hide|'):
        hid=int(data.split('|')[1]); return await _student_homework_panel(q,hid)
    if not _is_admin(update): return
    parts=data.split('|'); action=parts[0]; did=int(parts[1]) if len(parts)>1 and parts[1].isdigit() else None
    if did and (not _draft(did) or int(_draft(did)['admin_telegram_id'])!=update.effective_user.id): return
    if action=='hw2_step1': return await _show_step1(q,did)
    if action=='hw2_text': context.user_data['hw2_wait']={'draft':did,'kind':'text'}; return await q.edit_message_text(_draft_text(_draft(did),1)+'\n\n✍️ Отправьте текст задания одним сообщением.\n\n[Черновик уже сохранён автоматически.]')
    if action=='hw2_file': context.user_data['hw2_wait']={'draft':did,'kind':'file'}; return await q.edit_message_text(_draft_text(_draft(did),1)+'\n\n📎 Отправьте документ или фото. Можно отправить несколько по одному. После каждого файла вернёмся сюда.')
    if action=='hw2_step2': return await _show_step2(q,did)
    if action=='hw2_grade': return await _show_topics(q,did,parts[2],int(parts[3]))
    if action=='hw2_topic':
        _,_,s,g,t=parts; _touch(did,topic_grade=int(g),topic_subject=s,topic_key=t,formula_topics_json='[]',formula_decision=None); return await _show_step3(q,did)
    if action=='hw2_repeat_grades': return await q.edit_message_text('🔁 ТЕМА НА ПОВТОРЕНИЕ\n\nВыберите класс материала.',reply_markup=_repeat_grade_markup(did))
    if action=='hw2_rgrade': return await _repeat_topic_picker(q,did,int(parts[2]),_draft(did)['subject'])
    if action=='hw2_rtopic':
        _,_,g,s,t=parts; d=_draft(did); arr=json.loads(d['repeat_topics_json'] or '[]'); key=f'{g}|{s}|{t}';
        if key not in arr: arr.append(key)
        _touch(did,repeat_topics_json=json.dumps(arr,ensure_ascii=False)); return await _show_step3(q,did)
    if action=='hw2_topic_search': context.user_data['hw2_wait']={'draft':did,'kind':'topic_search'}; return await q.edit_message_text('🔎 ПОИСК ТЕМЫ\n\nНапишите название: например «закон Ома» или «термодинамика».')
    if action=='hw2_formula_search': context.user_data['hw2_wait']={'draft':did,'kind':'formula_search'}; return await q.edit_message_text('🔎 ПОИСК ФОРМУЛЫ\n\nНапишите название, величину или формулу, например I = U/R.')
    if action=='hw2_formula_yes': _touch(did,formula_decision=1); return await _show_step3(q,did)
    if action=='hw2_formula_no': _touch(did,formula_decision=0,formula_topics_json='[]'); return await _show_step3(q,did)
    if action=='hw2_formula_toggle':
        _,_,g,s,t=parts; d=_draft(did); arr=json.loads(d['formula_topics_json'] or '[]'); key=f'{g}|{s}|{t}'; arr.remove(key) if key in arr else arr.append(key); _touch(did,formula_decision=1,formula_topics_json=json.dumps(arr,ensure_ascii=False)); return await _show_step3(q,did)
    if action=='hw2_formula_grades': return await q.edit_message_text('🧮 ФОРМУЛЫ • ВЫБЕРИТЕ КЛАСС\n\nМожно прикрепить формулу прошлых лет.',reply_markup=_formula_grade_markup(did))
    if action=='hw2_fgrade': return await _formula_topic_picker(q,did,int(parts[2]),_draft(did)['subject'])
    if action=='hw2_ftopic':
        _,_,g,s,t=parts; d=_draft(did);arr=json.loads(d['formula_topics_json'] or '[]');key=f'{g}|{s}|{t}';
        if key not in arr:arr.append(key)
        _touch(did,formula_decision=1,formula_topics_json=json.dumps(arr,ensure_ascii=False));return await _show_step3(q,did)
    if action=='hw2_step4': return await _show_step4(q,did)
    if action=='hw2_deadline':
        choice=parts[2]; d=_draft(did)
        if choice=='next': val=_next_lesson_deadline(d['user_id'],d['lesson_id']) or (datetime.now()+timedelta(days=7)).isoformat(timespec='minutes')
        elif choice=='none': val='NO_DEADLINE'
        else: val=(datetime.now()+timedelta(days=int(choice))).replace(hour=20,minute=0,second=0,microsecond=0).isoformat()
        _touch(did,deadline=val); return await _show_review(q,did)
    if action=='hw2_review': return await _show_review(q,did)
    if action=='hw2_ai_topic': return await _ai_topic(q,did,context)
    if action=='hw2_ai_use':
        _,_,g,s,t=parts;_touch(did,topic_grade=int(g),topic_subject=s,topic_key=t,formula_decision=None,formula_topics_json='[]');return await _show_step3(q,did)
    if action=='hw2_send': return await _finalize(q,did,context)
    if action=='hw2_cancel': _touch(did,status='CANCELLED'); return await q.edit_message_text('❌ Черновик ДЗ отменён.',reply_markup=InlineKeyboardMarkup([[InlineKeyboardButton('🏠 Главная',callback_data='admin_main')]]))


def _repeat_grade_markup(did):
    rows=[]
    for i in range(7,12,3): rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton(f'{g} класс',callback_data=f'hw2_rgrade|{did}|{g}') for g in range(i,min(i+3,12))])
    rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton('⬅️ Назад',callback_data=f'hw2_step3|{did}')]); return InlineKeyboardMarkup(rows)

async def _repeat_topic_picker(q,did,g,s):
    topics=FORMULA_LIBRARY.get(g,{}).get(s,{})
    rows=[[InlineKeyboardButton((v.get('title') or k)[:55],callback_data=f'hw2_rtopic|{did}|{g}|{s}|{k}')] for k,v in topics.items()]
    rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton('⬅️ К классам',callback_data=f'hw2_repeat_grades|{did}')])
    await q.edit_message_text(f'🔁 ПОВТОРЕНИЕ • {g} КЛАСС\n\nВыберите тему:',reply_markup=InlineKeyboardMarkup(rows))

def _search_topics(query, subject=None, limit=12):
    q=(query or '').lower().replace('ё','е'); out=[]
    for g,subs in FORMULA_LIBRARY.items():
        for s,topics in subs.items():
            if subject and s!=subject: continue
            for t,item in topics.items():
                hay=((item.get('title') or '')+' '+(item.get('text') or '')).lower().replace('ё','е')
                score=sum(1 for token in q.split() if token in hay)
                if q in hay: score+=5
                if score: out.append((score,g,s,t,item.get('title') or t))
    return sorted(out,reverse=True)[:limit]

def _formula_grade_markup(did):
    rows=[]
    for i in range(7,12,3): rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton(f'{g} класс',callback_data=f'hw2_fgrade|{did}|{g}') for g in range(i,min(i+3,12))])
    rows.append([InlineKeyboardButton('⬅️ Назад',callback_data=f'hw2_step3|{did}')]);return InlineKeyboardMarkup(rows)

        lines += [
            "",
            f"⏰ Срок: {deadline}",
        ]

    if mode != "HOME":
        lines += [
            "",
            "━━━━━━━━━━━━",
        ]

    if extra:
        lines.append(
            str(extra)
        )

    if _hw_done(hw):
        lines += [
            "",
            "✅ ДЗ выполнено",
        ]

    # Telegram media caption limit.
    return "\n".join(lines)[:950]


def _hw_card_markup(
    hid,
    links=None,
    mode="HOME",
    extra_rows=None,
    done=False,
):
    links = links or []

    rows = []

    if mode == "HOME":
        rows.append([
            InlineKeyboardButton(
                "📚 Формулы"
                + (
                    f" ({len(links)})"
                    if links
                    else ""
                ),
                callback_data=(
                    f"hw2_student_formula|{hid}"
                ),
            ),
            InlineKeyboardButton(
                "🔎 Ещё",
                callback_data=(
                    f"hw2_student_browse|{hid}"
                ),
            ),
        ])

    if extra_rows:
        rows.extend(
            extra_rows
        )

    if mode != "HOME":
        rows.append([
            InlineKeyboardButton(
                "📄 ДЗ",
                callback_data=(
                    f"hw2_student_home|{hid}"
                ),
            ),
            InlineKeyboardButton(
                "📚 Формулы",
                callback_data=(
                    f"hw2_student_formula|{hid}"
                ),
            ),
        ])

    # ALWAYS visible.
    rows.append([
        InlineKeyboardButton(
            (
                "✅ ДЗ выполнено"
                if done
                else "✅ Выполнено"
            ),
            callback_data=(
                f"hw2_student_home|{hid}"
                if done
                else f"hw2_student_done|{hid}"
            ),
        )
    ])

    return InlineKeyboardMarkup(
        rows
    )


async def _edit_hw_card(
    q,
    text,
    markup,
):
    if (
        q.message.document
        or q.message.photo
        or q.message.video
        or q.message.audio
        or q.message.animation
    ):
        await q.edit_message_caption(
            caption=text[:1000],
            reply_markup=markup,
        )
    else:
        await q.edit_message_text(
            text[:3900],
            reply_markup=markup,
        )


async def _send_hw_card(
    bot,
    chat_id,
    hid,
):
    """
    Open homework = re-send the actual attachment.

    Telegram file_id is canonical for Telegram delivery:
    we do not download/re-upload a PDF merely because
    the student opened homework from the menu again.
    """

    hw = DB.get_homework(
        int(hid)
    )

    if not hw:
        return None

    attachments = _attachments(
        hid=hid
    )

    links = _hw_links(
        hid
    )

    text = _hw_card_text(
        hw
    )

    markup = _hw_card_markup(
        hid,
        links=links,
        done=_hw_done(hw),
    )

    # Text-only homework is valid.
    if not attachments:
        return await bot.send_message(
            int(chat_id),
            text,
            reply_markup=markup,
        )

    async def send_one(
        attachment,
        *,
        primary=False,
    ):
        keys = attachment.keys()

        # Prefer the canonical Telegram id.
        telegram_file_id = (
            attachment["telegram_file_id"]
            if (
                "telegram_file_id" in keys
                and attachment["telegram_file_id"]
            )
            else (
                attachment["file_id"]
                if (
                    "file_id" in keys
                    and attachment["file_id"]
                )
                else None
            )
        )

        file_type = str(
            (
                attachment["file_type"]
                if "file_type" in keys
                else None
            )
            or (
                attachment["kind"]
                if "kind" in keys
                else None
            )
            or "document"
        ).lower()

        filename = (
            attachment["file_name"]
            if (
                "file_name" in keys
                and attachment["file_name"]
            )
            else (
                attachment["filename"]
                if (
                    "filename" in keys
                    and attachment["filename"]
                )
                else "homework"
            )
        )

        caption = (
            text
            if primary
            else (
                f"📎 {filename}"
            )
        )

        reply_markup = (
            markup
            if primary
            else None
        )

        if not telegram_file_id:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Telegram file_id отсутствует"
            )

        # PHOTO
        if file_type == "photo":
            return await bot.send_photo(
                int(chat_id),
                photo=telegram_file_id,
                caption=caption[:1000],
                reply_markup=reply_markup,
            )

        # Everything else is delivered as a real document.
        #
        # IMPORTANT:
        # PDF is NOT converted to text/link.
        # Telegram receives its existing file_id and displays
        # the document/preview natively.
        return await bot.send_document(
            int(chat_id),
            document=telegram_file_id,
            caption=caption[:1000],
            reply_markup=reply_markup,
        )

    primary = attachments[0]

    try:
        message = await send_one(
            primary,
            primary=True,
        )

    except Exception as exc:
        print(
            "HOMEWORK PRIMARY FILE DIRECT SEND ERROR:",
            hid,
            repr(exc),
            flush=True,
        )

        # Fallback specifically for historic Telegram PDF/document ids.
        # If Telegram can still download it, re-upload once.
        keys = primary.keys()

        fid = (
            primary["telegram_file_id"]
            if (
                "telegram_file_id" in keys
                and primary["telegram_file_id"]
            )
            else (
                primary["file_id"]
                if (
                    "file_id" in keys
                    and primary["file_id"]
                )
                else None
            )
        )

        filename = (
            primary["file_name"]
            if (
                "file_name" in keys
                and primary["file_name"]
            )
            else (
                primary["filename"]
                if (
                    "filename" in keys
                    and primary["filename"]
                )
                else "homework"
            )
        )

        if fid:
            try:
                from vk_adapter.attachment_bridge import (
                    telegram_file_bytes,
                )

                content = telegram_file_bytes(
                    fid
                )

                message = await bot.send_document(
                    int(chat_id),
                    document=InputFile(
                        BytesIO(content),
                        filename=filename,
                    ),
                    caption=text[:1000],
                    reply_markup=markup,
                )

            except Exception as fallback_exc:
                print(
                    "HOMEWORK PRIMARY FILE FALLBACK ERROR:",
                    hid,
                    repr(fallback_exc),
                    flush=True,
                )

                message = await bot.send_message(
                    int(chat_id),
                    text
                    + (
                        "\n\n⚠️ Старый файл больше "
                        "не удалось получить. "
                        "Преподавателю нужно "
                        "прикрепить его заново."
                    ),
                    reply_markup=markup,
                )

        else:
            message = await bot.send_message(
                int(chat_id),
                text
                + (
                    "\n\n⚠️ У этого старого ДЗ "
                    "не сохранён файл. "
                    "Преподавателю нужно "
                    "прикрепить его заново."
                ),
                reply_markup=markup,
            )

    # Re-send every additional homework attachment too.
    #
    # They intentionally have no second control keyboard:
    # Formula/Done controls remain on the primary attachment.
    for extra in attachments[1:]:
        try:
            await send_one(
                extra,
                primary=False,
            )

        except Exception as exc:
            print(
                "HOMEWORK EXTRA FILE SEND ERROR:",
                hid,
                repr(exc),
                flush=True,
            )

    return message


def _hw_links(hid):
    c=DB.connect()
    try:return c.execute('SELECT * FROM homework_formula_links WHERE homework_id=? ORDER BY id',(int(hid),)).fetchall()
    finally:c.close()


async def _student_homework_panel(q,hid):
    hw = DB.get_homework(
        int(hid)
    )

    user = DB.get_user_by_telegram_id(
        q.from_user.id
    )

    if (
        not hw
        or not user
        or int(hw["user_id"])
        != int(user["id"])
    ):
        try:
            await q.answer(
                "Домашнее задание не найдено",
                show_alert=True,
            )
        except Exception:
            pass

        return

    links = _hw_links(
        hid
    )

    text = _hw_card_text(
        hw
    )

    markup = _hw_card_markup(
        hid,
        links=links,
        done=_hw_done(hw),
    )

    # If the student is already looking at the REAL FILE
    # and switches between homework/formulas,
    # keep the same message and only change its caption/buttons.
    if (
        q.message.document
        or q.message.photo
        or q.message.video
        or q.message.audio
        or q.message.animation
    ):
        return await _edit_hw_card(
            q,
            text,
            markup,
        )

    # IMPORTANT:
    # Opening from:
    #   - Homework menu
    #   - Homework list
    #   - old "Open homework" card
    # means RE-SEND THE ACTUAL FILE.
    await _send_hw_card(
        q.get_bot(),
        q.message.chat_id,
        int(hid),
    )

    # Remove only the old navigation/menu card,
    # not the newly sent homework file.
    try:
        await q.message.delete()
    except Exception:
        pass


async def _student_controls(
    context,
    chat_id,
    hid,
):
    """
    Legacy compatibility entry point.

    Controls now live directly under the real homework
    attachment, so no extra 'task opened' message is sent.
    """
    return None

=== 14. ATTACHMENT REPOSITORY SOURCE ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from typing import Iterable

import database


def initialize():
    conn = database.connect()
    try:
        conn.execute(
            """
            CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS homework_attachments (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                draft_id INTEGER,
                homework_id INTEGER,
                file_type TEXT,
                file_id TEXT,
                telegram_file_id TEXT,
                file_unique_id TEXT,
                file_name TEXT,
                caption TEXT,
                kind TEXT,
                filename TEXT,
                mime_type TEXT,
                vk_attachment_key TEXT,
                vk_url TEXT,
                source_channel TEXT,
                created_at TEXT,
                updated_at TEXT
            )
            """
        )

        cols = {
            row["name"]
            for row in conn.execute(
                "PRAGMA table_info(homework_attachments)"
            ).fetchall()
        }

        additions = {
            "draft_id": "INTEGER",
            "homework_id": "INTEGER",
            "file_type": "TEXT",
            "file_id": "TEXT",
            "telegram_file_id": "TEXT",
            "file_unique_id": "TEXT",
            "file_name": "TEXT",
            "caption": "TEXT",
            "kind": "TEXT",
            "filename": "TEXT",
            "mime_type": "TEXT",
            "vk_attachment_key": "TEXT",
            "vk_url": "TEXT",
            "source_channel": "TEXT",
            "created_at": "TEXT",
            "updated_at": "TEXT",
        }

        for name, ddl in additions.items():
            if name not in cols:
                conn.execute(
                    f"ALTER TABLE homework_attachments "
                    f"ADD COLUMN {name} {ddl}"
                )

        now = _now()

        conn.execute(
            """
            UPDATE homework_attachments
               SET kind=COALESCE(
                       NULLIF(kind,''),
                       CASE
                           WHEN file_type='photo' THEN 'photo'
                           ELSE 'document'
                       END
                   ),
                   file_type=COALESCE(
                       NULLIF(file_type,''),
                       CASE
                           WHEN kind='photo' THEN 'photo'
                           ELSE 'document'
                       END
                   ),
                   filename=COALESCE(
                       NULLIF(filename,''),
                       file_name
                   ),
                   file_name=COALESCE(
                       NULLIF(file_name,''),
                       filename
                   ),
                   telegram_file_id=COALESCE(
                       NULLIF(telegram_file_id,''),
                       file_id
                   ),
                   file_id=COALESCE(
                       NULLIF(file_id,''),
                       telegram_file_id
                   ),
                   created_at=COALESCE(created_at, ?),
                   updated_at=COALESCE(updated_at, ?)
            """,
            (now, now),
        )
        conn.execute(
            "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_homework_attachments_hw ON homework_attachments(homework_id,id)"
        )
        conn.commit()
    finally:
        conn.close()


def _now():
    return database.now_iso()


def _normalize_kind(value):
    value = str(value or "document").lower()
    return "photo" if value in {"photo", "image", "picture"} else "document"


def save_reference(
    homework_id: int,
    *,
    kind: str,
    filename: str | None = None,
    mime_type: str | None = None,
    telegram_file_id: str | None = None,
    vk_attachment_key: str | None = None,
    vk_url: str | None = None,
    source_channel: str | None = None,
):
    initialize()
    now = _now()
    conn = database.connect()
    try:
        existing = conn.execute(
            """
            SELECT id FROM homework_attachments
            WHERE homework_id=?
              AND kind=?
              AND COALESCE(telegram_file_id,'')=COALESCE(?, '')
              AND COALESCE(vk_attachment_key,'')=COALESCE(?, '')
              AND COALESCE(filename,'')=COALESCE(?, '')
            LIMIT 1
            """,
            (
                int(homework_id),
                _normalize_kind(kind),
                telegram_file_id,
                vk_attachment_key,
                filename,
            ),
        ).fetchone()
        if existing:
            conn.execute(
                """
                UPDATE homework_attachments
                SET mime_type=COALESCE(?,mime_type),
                    telegram_file_id=COALESCE(?,telegram_file_id),
                    vk_attachment_key=COALESCE(?,vk_attachment_key),
                    vk_url=COALESCE(?,vk_url),
                    source_channel=COALESCE(?,source_channel),
                    updated_at=?
                WHERE id=?
                """,
                (
                    mime_type,
                    telegram_file_id,
                    vk_attachment_key,
                    vk_url,
                    source_channel,
                    now,
                    int(existing["id"]),
                ),
            )
            conn.commit()
            return int(existing["id"])

        cur = conn.execute(
            """
            INSERT INTO homework_attachments(
                homework_id,
                kind,file_type,
                filename,file_name,
                mime_type,
                telegram_file_id,file_id,
                vk_attachment_key,vk_url,
                source_channel,created_at,updated_at
            ) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)
            """,
            (
                int(homework_id),
                _normalize_kind(kind),
                _normalize_kind(kind),
                filename,
                filename,
                mime_type,
                telegram_file_id,
                telegram_file_id,
                vk_attachment_key,
                vk_url,
                source_channel,
                now,
                now,
            ),
        )
        conn.commit()
        return int(cur.lastrowid)
    finally:
        conn.close()


def list_for_homework(homework_id: int):
    initialize()
    conn = database.connect()
    try:
        return list(
            conn.execute(
                "SELECT * FROM homework_attachments WHERE homework_id=? ORDER BY id",
                (int(homework_id),),
            ).fetchall()
        )
    finally:
        conn.close()


def count_for_homework(homework_id: int):
    initialize()
    conn = database.connect()
    try:
        return int(
            conn.execute(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM homework_attachments WHERE homework_id=?",
                (int(homework_id),),
            ).fetchone()["n"]
            or 0
        )
    finally:
        conn.close()


def save_vk_descriptors(homework_id: int, descriptors: Iterable[dict]):
    ids = []
    for item in descriptors or []:
        ids.append(
            save_reference(
                int(homework_id),
                kind=item.get("kind") or "document",
                filename=item.get("filename"),
                mime_type=item.get("content_type"),
                vk_attachment_key=item.get("vk_key"),
                vk_url=item.get("url"),
                source_channel="VK",
            )
        )
    return ids


def set_telegram_file_id(attachment_id: int, telegram_file_id: str):
    if not telegram_file_id:
        return
    initialize()
    conn = database.connect()
    try:
        conn.execute(
            """
            UPDATE homework_attachments
               SET telegram_file_id=?,
                   file_id=?,
                   updated_at=?
             WHERE id=?
            """,
            (
                str(telegram_file_id),
                str(telegram_file_id),
                _now(),
                int(attachment_id),
            ),
        )
        conn.commit()
    finally:
        conn.close()


def _extract_tg_file_id(result: dict, kind: str):
    if not isinstance(result, dict):
        return None
    message = result.get("result") or result
    if _normalize_kind(kind) == "photo":
        photos = message.get("photo") or []
        if photos:
            return photos[-1].get("file_id")
    doc = message.get("document") or {}
    return doc.get("file_id")


def _telegram_send_remote(chat_id: int, item, caption=""):
    from vk_adapter import attachment_bridge as bridge

    file_id = item["telegram_file_id"]
    method = "sendPhoto" if item["kind"] == "photo" else "sendDocument"
    field = "photo" if item["kind"] == "photo" else "document"
    if not bridge.BOT_TOKEN:
        return False

    try:
        if file_id:
            result = bridge._json_request(
                f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{bridge.BOT_TOKEN}/{method}",
                {
                    "chat_id": int(chat_id),
                    field: str(file_id),
                    "caption": str(caption or "")[:1000],
                },
            )
            return bool(result.get("ok"))

        if item["vk_url"]:
            content = bridge._bytes_request(item["vk_url"], timeout=90)
            ctype = item["mime_type"] or "image/jpeg" if item["kind"] == "photo" else "application/octet-stream"
            body, form_type = bridge._multipart(
                {"chat_id": int(chat_id), "caption": str(caption or "")[:1000]},
                [(field, item["filename"] or ("image.jpg" if item["kind"] == "photo" else "document"), content, ctype)],
            )
            result = bridge._json_request(
                f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{bridge.BOT_TOKEN}/{method}",
                body,
                {"Content-Type": form_type},
                timeout=90,
            )
            if result.get("ok"):
                new_id = _extract_tg_file_id(result, item["kind"])
                if new_id:
                    set_telegram_file_id(int(item["id"]), new_id)
                return True
    except Exception as exc:
        print("HOMEWORK ATTACHMENT TG SEND ERROR:", repr(exc))
    return False


def _vk_send_remote(peer_id: int, item, caption=""):
    from vk_adapter import attachment_bridge as bridge

    try:
        if item["vk_attachment_key"]:
            bridge._vk_api(
                "messages.send",
                peer_id=int(peer_id),
                random_id=__import__("random").randint(1, 2_000_000_000),
                message=str(caption or ""),
                attachment=str(item["vk_attachment_key"]),
            )
            return True

        if item["telegram_file_id"]:
            content = bridge.telegram_file_bytes(item["telegram_file_id"])
            key = (
                bridge._vk_upload_photo(peer_id, content, item["filename"] or "image.jpg", item["mime_type"] or "image/jpeg")
                if item["kind"] == "photo"
                else bridge._vk_upload_document(peer_id, content, item["filename"] or "document", item["mime_type"])
            )
            bridge._vk_api(
                "messages.send",
                peer_id=int(peer_id),
                random_id=__import__("random").randint(1, 2_000_000_000),
                message=str(caption or ""),
                attachment=key,
            )
            conn = database.connect()
            try:
                conn.execute(
                    "UPDATE homework_attachments SET vk_attachment_key=?,updated_at=? WHERE id=?",
                    (key, _now(), int(item["id"])),
                )
                conn.commit()
            finally:
                conn.close()
            return True
    except Exception as exc:
        print("HOMEWORK ATTACHMENT VK SEND ERROR:", repr(exc))
    return False


def resend_to_student(homework_id: int, caption=""):
    homework = database.get_homework(int(homework_id))
    if not homework:
        return {"telegram": 0, "vk": 0, "count": 0}
    user = database.get_user(int(homework["user_id"]))
    rows = list_for_homework(int(homework_id))
    result = {"telegram": 0, "vk": 0, "count": len(rows)}
    if not user:
        return result

    vk_peer = None
    try:
        from vk_adapter.service import vk_peer_for_user
        vk_peer = vk_peer_for_user(int(user["id"]))
    except Exception:
        pass

    for item in rows:
        if user["telegram_id"] and _telegram_send_remote(int(user["telegram_id"]), item, caption):
            result["telegram"] += 1
        if vk_peer and _vk_send_remote(int(vk_peer), item, caption):
            result["vk"] += 1
    return result


def send_to_admin_telegram(homework_id: int, chat_id: int, caption=""):
    rows = list_for_homework(int(homework_id))
    sent = 0
    for item in rows:
        if _telegram_send_remote(int(chat_id), item, caption):
            sent += 1
    return sent


def install_vk_bridge():
    """Persist refs whenever the existing VK homework delivery forwards attachments.

    Existing non-homework attachment forwarding is left unchanged. Homework id is
    recognized from the caption generated by vk_adapter.app ("ДЗ #123").
    """
    from vk_adapter import attachment_bridge as bridge

    current = bridge.vk_forward_to_student
    if getattr(current, "_homework_refs_installed", False):
        return

    def wrapped(user_id, attachments, caption=""):
        match = re.search(r"ДЗ\s*#(\d+)", str(caption or ""), re.I)
        if not match:
            return current(user_id, attachments, caption)

        homework_id = int(match.group(1))
        descriptors = bridge._descriptors(attachments)
        ids = save_vk_descriptors(homework_id, descriptors)
        result = {"telegram": 0, "vk": 0}
        user = database.get_user(int(user_id))
        peer = bridge._vk_peer(int(user_id))

        if peer and descriptors:
            try:
                result["vk"] = bridge._send_existing_vk(peer, descriptors, caption)
            except Exception as exc:
                print("HOMEWORK VK SEND ERROR:", repr(exc))

        if user and user["telegram_id"]:
            for attachment_id in ids:
                conn = database.connect()
                try:
                    row = conn.execute(
                        "SELECT * FROM homework_attachments WHERE id=?",
                        (int(attachment_id),),
                    ).fetchone()
                finally:
                    conn.close()
                if row and _telegram_send_remote(int(user["telegram_id"]), row, caption):
                    result["telegram"] += 1
        return result

    wrapped._homework_refs_installed = True
    bridge.vk_forward_to_student = wrapped


initialize()


def add_attachment(
    homework_id,
    file_id,
    file_unique_id=None,
    kind="document",
    filename=None,
    mime_type=None,
    source_channel="TELEGRAM",
):
    """
    Canonical lightweight attachment writer.

    Stores only Telegram/VK identifiers and metadata.
    Binary file contents are never stored in SQLite.
    """
    import database

    conn = database.connect()

    try:
        columns = {
            row["name"]
            for row in conn.execute(
                "PRAGMA table_info(homework_attachments)"
            ).fetchall()
        }

        now = database.now_iso()

        values = {
            "homework_id": int(homework_id),

            # New + legacy-compatible Telegram ids.
            "file_id": file_id,
            "telegram_file_id": file_id,
            "file_unique_id": file_unique_id,

            # New + legacy-compatible type/name.
            "kind": kind,
            "file_type": kind,
            "filename": filename,
            "file_name": filename,

            "mime_type": mime_type,

            # Cross-platform source metadata.
            "source_channel": source_channel,
            "source_platform": source_channel,

            "created_at": now,
            "updated_at": now,
        }

        keys = [
            key
            for key, value in values.items()
            if key in columns
            and value is not None
        ]

        if "homework_id" not in keys:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "homework_attachments.homework_id column missing"
            )

        sql = (
            "INSERT INTO homework_attachments ("
            + ",".join(keys)
            + ") VALUES ("
            + ",".join("?" for _ in keys)
            + ")"
        )

        cursor = conn.execute(
            sql,
            tuple(
                values[key]
                for key in keys
            ),
        )

        conn.commit()

        return int(cursor.lastrowid)

    finally:
        conn.close()

=== 16. ATTACHMENT BRIDGE SOURCE ===
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import mimetypes
import os
import random
import uuid
from urllib.parse import urlencode
from urllib.request import Request, urlopen

import database

BOT_TOKEN = os.getenv("BOT_TOKEN", "").strip()
VK_TOKEN = os.getenv("VK_TOKEN", "").strip()
VK_API_VERSION = os.getenv("VK_API_VERSION", "5.199").strip() or "5.199"


def _json_request(url, data=None, headers=None, timeout=40):
    body = None
    hdr = {"User-Agent": "TutorBot-Attachment/1.0"}
    if headers:
        hdr.update(headers)
    if data is not None:
        body = data if isinstance(data, (bytes, bytearray)) else urlencode(data).encode("utf-8")
        if not headers or "Content-Type" not in headers:
            hdr["Content-Type"] = "application/x-www-form-urlencoded"
    with urlopen(Request(url, data=body, headers=hdr), timeout=timeout) as resp:
        return json.loads(resp.read().decode("utf-8"))


def _bytes_request(url, timeout=60):
    with urlopen(Request(url, headers={"User-Agent": "TutorBot-Attachment/1.0"}), timeout=timeout) as resp:
        return resp.read()


def _multipart(fields, files):
    boundary = "----TutorBot" + uuid.uuid4().hex
    chunks = []
    for name, value in fields.items():
        chunks += [
            f"--{boundary}\r\n".encode(),
            f'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="{name}"\r\n\r\n'.encode(),
            str(value).encode("utf-8"),
            b"\r\n",
        ]
    for field, filename, content, content_type in files:
        chunks += [
            f"--{boundary}\r\n".encode(),
            f'Content-Disposition: form-data; name="{field}"; filename="{filename}"\r\n'.encode("utf-8"),
            f"Content-Type: {content_type}\r\n\r\n".encode(),
            content,
            b"\r\n",
        ]
    chunks.append(f"--{boundary}--\r\n".encode())
    return b"".join(chunks), f"multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}"


def _vk_api(method, **params):
    if not VK_TOKEN:
        raise RuntimeError("VK_TOKEN is not configured")
    payload = dict(params)
    payload["access_token"] = VK_TOKEN
    payload["v"] = VK_API_VERSION
    data = _json_request(f"https://api.vk.com/method/{method}", payload)
    if "error" in data:
        raise RuntimeError(f"VK API {method}: {data['error']}")
    return data.get("response")


def _vk_peer(user_id):
    c = database.connect()
    try:
        row = c.execute(
            "SELECT external_id FROM channel_identities WHERE user_id=? AND channel='VK' AND enabled=1 LIMIT 1",
            (int(user_id),),
        ).fetchone()
        return int(row["external_id"]) if row else None
    finally:
        c.close()


def telegram_file_bytes(file_id):
    if not BOT_TOKEN:
        raise RuntimeError("BOT_TOKEN is not configured")
    info = _json_request(
        f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{BOT_TOKEN}/getFile",
        {"file_id": str(file_id)},
    )
    if not info.get("ok"):
        raise RuntimeError(f"Telegram getFile failed: {info}")
    return _bytes_request(
        f"https://api.telegram.org/file/bot{BOT_TOKEN}/{info['result']['file_path']}"
    )


def telegram_send_bytes(chat_id, content, filename, caption="", as_photo=False, content_type=None):
    if not BOT_TOKEN or not chat_id:
        return False
    method = "sendPhoto" if as_photo else "sendDocument"
    field = "photo" if as_photo else "document"
    ctype = content_type or mimetypes.guess_type(filename)[0] or "application/octet-stream"
    body, form_type = _multipart(
        {"chat_id": int(chat_id), "caption": str(caption or "")[:1000]},
        [(field, filename, content, ctype)],
    )
    try:
        result = _json_request(
            f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{BOT_TOKEN}/{method}",
            body,
            {"Content-Type": form_type},
            timeout=60,
        )
        return bool(result.get("ok"))
    except Exception as exc:
        print("ATTACHMENT TELEGRAM SEND ERROR:", repr(exc))
        return False


def _vk_upload_document(peer_id, content, filename, content_type=None):
    server = _vk_api("docs.getMessagesUploadServer", peer_id=int(peer_id), type="doc")
    ctype = content_type or mimetypes.guess_type(filename)[0] or "application/octet-stream"
    body, form_type = _multipart({}, [("file", filename, content, ctype)])
    uploaded = _json_request(server["upload_url"], body, {"Content-Type": form_type}, timeout=90)
    saved = _vk_api("docs.save", file=uploaded["file"], title=filename)
    doc = (saved.get("doc") if isinstance(saved, dict) else None) or (
        saved[0] if isinstance(saved, list) and saved else None
    )
    if not doc:
        raise RuntimeError(f"VK docs.save returned no document: {saved}")
    key = f"doc{doc['owner_id']}_{doc['id']}"
    if doc.get("access_key"):
        key += f"_{doc['access_key']}"
    return key


def _vk_upload_photo(peer_id, content, filename="image.jpg", content_type="image/jpeg"):
    server = _vk_api("photos.getMessagesUploadServer", peer_id=int(peer_id))
    body, form_type = _multipart({}, [("photo", filename, content, content_type or "image/jpeg")])
    uploaded = _json_request(server["upload_url"], body, {"Content-Type": form_type}, timeout=90)
    saved = _vk_api(
        "photos.saveMessagesPhoto",
        photo=uploaded["photo"],
        server=uploaded["server"],
        hash=uploaded["hash"],
    )
    photo = saved[0] if saved else None
    if not photo:
        raise RuntimeError("VK photo save returned no photo")
    key = f"photo{photo['owner_id']}_{photo['id']}"
    if photo.get("access_key"):
        key += f"_{photo['access_key']}"
    return key


def vk_send_bytes(peer_id, content, filename, caption="", as_photo=False, content_type=None):
    try:
        attachment = (
            _vk_upload_photo(peer_id, content, filename, content_type or "image/jpeg")
            if as_photo
            else _vk_upload_document(peer_id, content, filename, content_type)
        )
        _vk_api(
            "messages.send",
            peer_id=int(peer_id),
            random_id=random.randint(1, 2_000_000_000),
            message=str(caption or ""),
            attachment=attachment,
        )
        return True
    except Exception as exc:
        print("ATTACHMENT VK SEND ERROR:", repr(exc))
        return False


def telegram_to_student(user_id, file_id, filename, caption="", as_photo=False, content_type=None):
    content = telegram_file_bytes(file_id)
    user = database.get_user(int(user_id))
    result = {"telegram": False, "vk": False}
    if user and user["telegram_id"]:
        result["telegram"] = telegram_send_bytes(
            int(user["telegram_id"]), content, filename, caption, as_photo, content_type
        )
    peer = _vk_peer(int(user_id))
    if peer:
        result["vk"] = vk_send_bytes(peer, content, filename, caption, as_photo, content_type)
    return result


def vk_attachment_descriptor(item):
    kind = str(item.get("type") or "")
    obj = item.get(kind) or {}
    if kind == "doc":
        return {
            "kind": "document",
            "url": obj.get("url"),
            "filename": obj.get("title") or f"document_{obj.get('id', '')}",
            "content_type": mimetypes.guess_type(obj.get("title") or "")[0] or "application/octet-stream",
            "vk_key": "doc%s_%s%s"
            % (
                obj.get("owner_id"),
                obj.get("id"),
                ("_" + str(obj.get("access_key"))) if obj.get("access_key") else "",
            ),
        }
    if kind == "photo":
        sizes = obj.get("sizes") or []
        best = max(
            sizes,
            key=lambda x: int(x.get("width", 0)) * int(x.get("height", 0)),
            default={},
        )
        return {
            "kind": "photo",
            "url": best.get("url"),
            "filename": f"photo_{obj.get('id', '')}.jpg",
            "content_type": "image/jpeg",
            "vk_key": "photo%s_%s%s"
            % (
                obj.get("owner_id"),
                obj.get("id"),
                ("_" + str(obj.get("access_key"))) if obj.get("access_key") else "",
            ),
        }
    return None


def _descriptors(attachments):
    return [
        d
        for d in (vk_attachment_descriptor(x) for x in (attachments or []))
        if d and d.get("url")
    ]


def _send_existing_vk(peer, descriptors, caption=""):
    if not peer or not descriptors:
        return 0
    _vk_api(
        "messages.send",
        peer_id=int(peer),
        random_id=random.randint(1, 2_000_000_000),
        message=str(caption or ""),
        attachment=",".join(x["vk_key"] for x in descriptors if x.get("vk_key")),
    )
    return len(descriptors)


def vk_forward_to_student(user_id, attachments, caption=""):
    user = database.get_user(int(user_id))
    peer = _vk_peer(int(user_id))
    descriptors = _descriptors(attachments)
    result = {"telegram": 0, "vk": 0}
    if peer and descriptors:
        try:
            result["vk"] = _send_existing_vk(peer, descriptors, caption)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("ATTACHMENT VK FORWARD ERROR:", repr(exc))
    if user and user["telegram_id"]:
        for item in descriptors:
            try:
                content = _bytes_request(item["url"], timeout=90)
                if telegram_send_bytes(
                    int(user["telegram_id"]),
                    content,
                    item["filename"],
                    caption,
                    item["kind"] == "photo",
                    item["content_type"],
                ):
                    result["telegram"] += 1
            except Exception as exc:
                print("ATTACHMENT VK->TG ERROR:", repr(exc))
    return result


def vk_forward_to_admin_vk(attachments, caption=""):
    from . import schema

    descriptors = _descriptors(attachments)
    sent = 0
    for peer in schema.get_admin_external_ids("VK"):
        try:
            sent += _send_existing_vk(int(peer), descriptors, caption)
        except Exception as exc:
            print("ATTACHMENT VK->ADMIN VK ERROR:", repr(exc))
    return sent


def vk_forward_to_admin_telegram(attachments, caption=""):
    """Mirror one student VK attachment set to all active teacher channels."""
    from core import admin_roles

    descriptors = _descriptors(attachments)
    tg_sent = 0
    for admin in admin_roles.list_admins():
        if not admin["active"] or not admin["telegram_id"]:
            continue
        for item in descriptors:
            try:
                content = _bytes_request(item["url"], timeout=90)
                if telegram_send_bytes(
                    int(admin["telegram_id"]),
                    content,
                    item["filename"],
                    caption,
                    item["kind"] == "photo",
                    item["content_type"],
                ):
                    tg_sent += 1
            except Exception as exc:
                print("ATTACHMENT VK->ADMIN TG ERROR:", repr(exc))

    vk_sent = vk_forward_to_admin_vk(attachments, caption)
    return {"telegram": tg_sent, "vk": vk_sent}

=== 21. TASK ADMIN SOURCE ===
from __future__ import annotations
from telegram import InlineKeyboardButton, InlineKeyboardMarkup, Update
from telegram.ext import CallbackQueryHandler, ConversationHandler, MessageHandler, ContextTypes, filters
from core import admin_roles, teacher_tasks
import database

WAIT_TITLE=1301

def _admin(uid): return admin_roles.is_admin_id(uid)

async def _edit(q,text,kb=None):
    try: await q.edit_message_text(text[:3900],reply_markup=kb)
    except Exception as e:
        if 'Message is not modified' not in str(e): raise

def _home_markup():
    teacher_tasks.sync_auto(); rows=teacher_tasks.list_open(15); kb=[]
    for r in rows:
        icon='🔴' if int(r['priority'] or 2)==1 else ('🟡' if int(r['priority'] or 2)==2 else '🟢')
        kb.append([InlineKeyboardButton(f"{icon} {str(r['title'])[:42]}",callback_data=f"task_view|{r['id']}")])
    kb += [[InlineKeyboardButton('➕ Добавить задачу',callback_data='task_add')],
           [InlineKeyboardButton('🔄 Обновить умные задачи',callback_data='task_sync')],
           [InlineKeyboardButton('⬅️ Назад',callback_data='admin_main'),InlineKeyboardButton('🏠 Главная',callback_data='global_home')]]
    return InlineKeyboardMarkup(kb),len(rows)

async def callbacks(update:Update,context:ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    q=update.callback_query
    if not q or not q.data.startswith('task_'): return
    if not _admin(q.from_user.id): await q.answer('Нет доступа',show_alert=True); return
    await q.answer(); d=q.data
    if d in ('task_home','task_sync'):
        kb,n=_home_markup(); await _edit(q,f'📌 МОИ ЗАДАЧИ\n\n💜 Я собрала рабочие дела в одном месте. Открыто: {n}.\n\nАвтоматические задачи создаются без ИИ: оплаты, просроченные ДЗ и отсутствие следующей записи.',kb); return
    if d=='task_add':
        context.user_data['task_user_id']=None
        await _edit(q,'📌 НОВАЯ ЗАДАЧА\n\nНапишите одним сообщением, что нужно сделать.\n\nНапример: «Написать маме Ивана после занятия».',InlineKeyboardMarkup([[InlineKeyboardButton('❌ Отмена',callback_data='task_home')]])); return WAIT_TITLE
    if d.startswith('task_add_user|'):
        context.user_data['task_user_id']=int(d.split('|')[1]); u=database.get_user(context.user_data['task_user_id']); name=u['name'] if u else 'ученику'
        await _edit(q,f'📌 ЗАДАЧА • {name}\n\nНапишите, что нужно сделать.',InlineKeyboardMarkup([[InlineKeyboardButton('❌ Отмена',callback_data=f'admin_student|{context.user_data["task_user_id"]}')]])); return WAIT_TITLE
    if d.startswith('task_view|'):
        tid=int(d.split('|')[1]); r=teacher_tasks.get(tid)
        if not r: await _edit(q,'💜 Эта задача уже недоступна.',InlineKeyboardMarkup([[InlineKeyboardButton('⬅️ К задачам',callback_data='task_home')]])); return
        who=f"\n👤 {r['user_name']}" if r['user_name'] else ''
        due=f"\n🕐 {str(r['due_at'])[:16].replace('T',' ')}" if r['due_at'] else ''
        kb=[]
        if r['user_id']: kb.append([InlineKeyboardButton('👤 Карточка ученика',callback_data=f"admin_student|{r['user_id']}"),InlineKeyboardButton('💜 Аделия',callback_data=f"mh_student_ask|{r['user_id']}")])
        kb += [[InlineKeyboardButton('✅ Выполнено',callback_data=f'task_done|{tid}'),InlineKeyboardButton('🕐 На завтра',callback_data=f'task_defer|{tid}')],[InlineKeyboardButton('⬅️ К задачам',callback_data='task_home'),InlineKeyboardButton('🏠 Главная',callback_data='global_home')]]
        await _edit(q,f"📌 ЗАДАЧА{who}{due}\n\n{r['title']}",InlineKeyboardMarkup(kb)); return
    if d.startswith('task_done|'):
        teacher_tasks.set_status(int(d.split('|')[1]),'DONE'); kb,n=_home_markup(); await _edit(q,f'💜 Готово. Закрыла задачу.\n\nОсталось: {n}.',kb); return
    if d.startswith('task_defer|'):
        teacher_tasks.defer(int(d.split('|')[1]),24); kb,n=_home_markup(); await _edit(q,f'💜 Отложила до завтра. Она не потеряется.\n\nСейчас в очереди: {n}.',kb); return

async def title_text(update:Update,context:ContextTypes.DEFAULT_TYPE):
    if not _admin(update.effective_user.id): return ConversationHandler.END
    title=(update.message.text or '').strip()
    if len(title)<2:
        await update.message.reply_text('💜 Напишите задачу чуть подробнее.'); return WAIT_TITLE
    uid=context.user_data.pop('task_user_id',None); teacher_tasks.add(title,user_id=uid)
    await update.message.reply_text('💜 Готово. Добавила задачу.',reply_markup=InlineKeyboardMarkup([[InlineKeyboardButton('📌 Мои задачи',callback_data='task_home')],[InlineKeyboardButton('🏠 Главная',callback_data='global_home')]])); return ConversationHandler.END

def handlers():
    conv=ConversationHandler(entry_points=[CallbackQueryHandler(callbacks,pattern=r'^task_(?:add|add_user\|\d+)$')],states={WAIT_TITLE:[MessageHandler(filters.TEXT & ~filters.COMMAND,title_text)]},fallbacks=[],allow_reentry=True)
    return [conv,CallbackQueryHandler(callbacks,pattern=r'^task_')]

=== 27. HOMEWORK / TASK / LESSON DB SCHEMA ===

================================================
TABLE lessons
================================================
CREATE SQL:
CREATE TABLE lessons (

            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

            user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,

            study_goal TEXT NOT NULL,
            subject TEXT NOT NULL,

            start_time TEXT NOT NULL,
            end_time TEXT NOT NULL,

            duration_minutes INTEGER NOT NULL,

            hourly_rate INTEGER,
            price INTEGER NOT NULL,

            -- UNPAID
            -- WAITING_RECEIPT
            -- WAITING_CONFIRMATION
            -- PAID
            payment_status TEXT DEFAULT 'UNPAID',

            payment_id INTEGER,
            package_id INTEGER,

            -- SCHEDULED
            -- COMPLETED
            -- NO_SHOW
            -- LATE_CANCELLED
            -- CANCELLED_BY_STUDENT
            -- CANCELLED_BY_TUTOR
            -- SICKNESS
            lesson_status TEXT DEFAULT 'SCHEDULED',

            -- Списалось ли оплаченное занятие
            charged INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

            sickness_number_in_month INTEGER,

            -- EXCUSED / CHARGED / NULL
            sickness_admin_decision TEXT,

            is_trial INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
            is_regular INTEGER DEFAULT 0,

            google_event_id TEXT,

            tutor_comment TEXT,

            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            updated_at TEXT, request_source TEXT DEFAULT 'STUDENT', requested_at TEXT, approved_at TEXT, rejected_at TEXT, started_at TEXT, finished_at TEXT, actual_duration_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 0, extra_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 0, attendance_status TEXT, cancelled_at TEXT, cancellation_reason TEXT, homework_required INTEGER DEFAULT 0, homework_assigned INTEGER DEFAULT 0, homework_skipped INTEGER DEFAULT 0, homework_reminder_sent INTEGER DEFAULT 0, bonus_minutes_processed INTEGER DEFAULT 0, payment_deadline TEXT, auto_cancelled_for_nonpayment INTEGER DEFAULT 0, rescheduled_from_lesson_id INTEGER, rescheduled_to_lesson_id INTEGER, reschedule_type TEXT, rescheduled_at TEXT, sickness_comment TEXT, sickness_requested_at TEXT, regular_schedule_id INTEGER, underworked_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 0, meeting_url TEXT, meeting_link_sent_at TEXT, program_id INTEGER, tutor_delay_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 0, tutor_delay_notified_at TEXT, reserved_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 0, reserved_paid_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 0, reserved_bonus_minutes INTEGER DEFAULT 0, post_lesson_reviewed INTEGER DEFAULT 0, teacher_planned_minutes INTEGER, payment_offer_sent_at TEXT, payment_offer_count INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, allow_unpaid INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, auto_cancel_admin_decision TEXT,

            FOREIGN KEY (user_id)
                REFERENCES users(id)
                ON DELETE CASCADE
        )

TABLE homework
================================================
CREATE SQL:
CREATE TABLE homework (

            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

            user_id INTEGER NOT NULL,

            lesson_id INTEGER,

            study_goal TEXT NOT NULL,
            subject TEXT NOT NULL,

            title TEXT,
            description TEXT,

            -- MAIN / EXTRA
            homework_type TEXT DEFAULT 'MAIN',

            -- RESHU_EGE
            -- RESHU_OGE
            -- CUSTOM
            source_type TEXT DEFAULT 'CUSTOM',

            source_reference TEXT,
            source_url TEXT,

            deadline TEXT,

            -- ASSIGNED
            -- UNDER_REVIEW
            -- REVIEWED
            -- REVISION
            status TEXT DEFAULT 'ASSIGNED',

            assigned_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            submitted_at TEXT,
            reviewed_at TEXT,

            created_at TEXT NOT NULL,
            updated_at TEXT, program_id INTEGER,

            FOREIGN KEY (user_id)
                REFERENCES users(id)
                ON DELETE CASCADE,

            FOREIGN KEY (lesson_id)
                REFERENCES lessons(id)
                ON DELETE SET NULL
        )

=== 40. TARGET CONTRACT ===
HOMEWORK V5 TARGET

1. Exactly one student homework callback owner.
2. homework_view|ID is compatibility alias only.
3. Menu/list/formula return/initial assignment use same renderer.
4. Real files remain real files.
5. No binary PDF/JPEG blobs in SQLite.
6. Telegram file_id and VK attachment ids remain metadata only.
7. PDF page 1 renders immediately.
8. Other PDF pages render only on demand.
9. Previous/current/next pager.
10. Reuse Telegram preview file_id after first render.
11. Temporary files always deleted.
12. Render concurrency bounded.
13. Rapid repeated clicks bounded.
14. Original PDF remains available.
15. Formula/search/done controls remain available.
16. Extra and normal homework use same delivery pipeline.
17. Completed lesson + homework_required=1
    + homework_assigned=0
    + homework_skipped=0
    => actionable Assign Homework task.
18. If teacher selected Без ДЗ:
    no task is created.
19. Task opens exact lesson/student/program homework creation.
20. Task closes only after real homework creation
    or explicit later Без ДЗ.
21. Tasks/day review/close day derive from same lesson state.
22. No duplicate homework task per lesson.
23. One backup + one migration + one consolidated patch.

============================================================
 END FINAL DIAGNOSTIC
============================================================
